APDC_cabinet_clear_selected_slot = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 1 } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_1 = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 2 } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_2 = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 3 } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_3 = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 4 } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_4 = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 5 } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_5 = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 6 } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_6 = yes }
}
APDC_cabinet_dismiss_selected_member = {
	APDC_cabinet_clear_selected_slot = yes
	APDC_update_cabinet_statistics = yes
}
APDC_cabinet_refresh_selected_candidate = {
	clear_variable = APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 1 } has_variable = APDC_cabinet_slot_1 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate = APDC_cabinet_slot_1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 2 } has_variable = APDC_cabinet_slot_2 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate = APDC_cabinet_slot_2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 3 } has_variable = APDC_cabinet_slot_3 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate = APDC_cabinet_slot_3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 4 } has_variable = APDC_cabinet_slot_4 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate = APDC_cabinet_slot_4 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 5 } has_variable = APDC_cabinet_slot_5 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate = APDC_cabinet_slot_5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_slot = 6 } has_variable = APDC_cabinet_slot_6 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_selected_candidate = APDC_cabinet_slot_6 }
	}
}

	if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 0 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 8 } } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_1 = yes }
	if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_2 > 0 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_2 < 8 } } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_2 = yes }
	if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_3 > 0 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_3 < 8 } } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_3 = yes }

	if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_6 > 0 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_6 < 8 } } } APDC_cabinet_clear_slot_6 = yes }
	APDC_update_cabinet_statistics = yes

APDC_rebuild_cabinet_pie = {
	clear_array = APDC_cabinet_pie
	resize_array = { array = APDC_cabinet_pie value = 1 size = 100 }
	set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_last_index = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cumulative = 0 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cumulative = APDC_coalition_influence_communism }
	set_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_pie_cumulative }
	multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff = 100 }
	round_temp_variable = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff
	clamp_variable = { var = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff min = 0 max = 100 }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = APDC_cabinet_pie_last_index
		end = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie^v = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_last_index = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff }
	add_to_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cumulative = APDC_coalition_influence_socialism }
	set_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_pie_cumulative }
	multiply_temp_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff = 100 }
	round_temp_variable = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff
	clamp_variable = { var = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff min = 0 max = 100 }

		start = APDC_cabinet_pie_last_index
		end = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie^v = 2 }
	}
	set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_pie_last_index = APDC_cabinet_pie_cutoff }

APDC_update_cabinet_statistics = {
APDC_add_current_ruling_party_to_governing_coalition = yes
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_communism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_socialism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_social_democracy = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_conservatism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_liberalism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_authoritarianism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_dictatorship = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_fascism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_fundamentalism = 0 }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 0 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 8 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_communism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 7 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 13 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_socialism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 12 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 18 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_social_democracy = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 17 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 23 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_conservatism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 22 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 28 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_liberalism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 27 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 33 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_authoritarianism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 32 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 38 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_dictatorship = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 37 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 43 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_fascism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 > 42 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_1 < 48 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_fundamentalism = 1 } }
if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_2 > 0 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_2 < 8 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_communism = 1 } }

if = { limit = { AND = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_2 > 32 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_slot_2 < 38 } } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_used_dictatorship = 1 } }

		set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_fundamentalism = party_popularity@fundamentalism }
		clamp_variable = { var = APDC_coalition_influence_fundamentalism min = 0 max = 1 }
		add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_total = APDC_coalition_influence_fundamentalism }
	}
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism = APDC_coalition_influence_communism }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism = APDC_coalition_influence_socialism }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy = APDC_coalition_influence_social_democracy }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism = APDC_coalition_influence_conservatism }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism = APDC_coalition_influence_liberalism }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism = APDC_coalition_influence_authoritarianism }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship = APDC_coalition_influence_dictatorship }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism = APDC_coalition_influence_fascism }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism = APDC_coalition_influence_fundamentalism }
if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_total > 0 } }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_communism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_socialism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_social_democracy = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_conservatism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_liberalism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_authoritarianism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_dictatorship = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_fascism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
		divide_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_fundamentalism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_total }
	}
else = {
		if = { limit = { has_government = communism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = socialism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = social_democracy } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = conservatism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = liberalism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = authoritarianism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = dictatorship } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_dictatorship = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = fascism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = fundamentalism } set_variable = { APDC_coalition_influence_fundamentalism = 1 } }
	}
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 0 }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 } }
if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count < 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_government = communism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = socialism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = social_democracy } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = conservatism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = liberalism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = authoritarianism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = dictatorship } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = fascism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { has_government = fundamentalism } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = 1 } }
	}
if = {
		# At six coalition parties or fewer, everyone receives one guaranteed seat.
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count < 7 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats = 6 }
		subtract_from_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats = APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count }
	}
else = {
		# More than six parties: build the true top-six band by counting how
		# many coalition parties have strictly greater influence than each party.
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship = 1 } }

		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism = 1 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism = 1 } }

		# A party is in the top-six band when fewer than six parties outrank it.
		# The band can contain more than six parties only when the cutoff is tied.
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_communism = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_socialism = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_social_democracy = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_conservatism = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_liberalism = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_authoritarianism = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_dictatorship = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fascism = 0 }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fundamentalism = 0 }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_communism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_communism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_socialism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_socialism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_socialism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_social_democracy_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_social_democracy < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_social_democracy = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_conservatism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_conservatism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_conservatism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_liberalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_liberalism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_liberalism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_authoritarianism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_authoritarianism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_authoritarianism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_dictatorship_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_dictatorship < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_dictatorship = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fascism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fascism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fascism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }
		if = { limit = { APDC_is_fundamentalism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_higher_influence_count_fundamentalism < 6 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fundamentalism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count = 1 } }

		# The weakest influence in the top-six band is the sixth-place cutoff.
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = 2 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_communism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_socialism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_social_democracy = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_conservatism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_liberalism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_authoritarianism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_dictatorship = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fascism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fundamentalism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism < APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 0 }
		if = {
			# Exactly six candidates means there is no contested cutoff.
			limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_count < 7 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_communism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_socialism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_social_democracy = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_conservatism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_liberalism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_authoritarianism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_dictatorship = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fascism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fundamentalism = 1 } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
		}
		else = {
			# When the cutoff is tied, only parties strictly above it get a seat.
			# Every contested cutoff seat is forwarded to the next allocation stage.
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_communism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_socialism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_socialism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_social_democracy = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_social_democracy > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_conservatism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_conservatism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_liberalism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_liberalism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_authoritarianism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_authoritarianism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_dictatorship = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_dictatorship > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fascism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fascism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_top_six_candidate_fundamentalism = 1 } check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_fundamentalism > APDC_cabinet_top_six_cutoff } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count = 1 } }
		}
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats = 6 }
		subtract_from_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats = APDC_cabinet_guaranteed_seat_count }
	}
clamp_variable = { var = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats min = 0 max = 6 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_count = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_communism = 0 }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_test_communism = APDC_cabinet_raw_influence_communism }
multiply_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_test_communism = APDC_cabinet_coalition_party_count }
if = { limit = { APDC_is_communism_in_governing_coalition = yes check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_test_communism > APDC_cabinet_coalition_total } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_communism = 1 } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_count = 1 } }

set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 0 }
if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_count > 0 } }
		set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats < APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold } } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 1 } }
		add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats < APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold } } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 2 } }
		add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats < APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold } } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 3 } }
		add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats < APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold } } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 4 } }
		add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats < APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold } } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 5 } }
		add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats < APDC_cabinet_extra_seat_threshold } } } set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party = 6 } }
	}
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_communism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_socialism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_social_democracy = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_conservatism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_liberalism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_authoritarianism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_dictatorship = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_fascism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
if = { limit = { check_variable = { APDC_cabinet_above_average_fundamentalism = 1 } } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party } }
set_variable = { APDC_cabinet_distributed_extra_seats = APDC_cabinet_extra_per_above_average_party }
multiply_variable = { APDC_cabinet_distributed_extra_seats = APDC_cabinet_above_average_count }
subtract_from_variable = { APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats = APDC_cabinet_distributed_extra_seats }
clamp_variable = { var = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats min = 0 max = 6 }
if = { limit = { has_government = communism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_communism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = socialism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_socialism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = social_democracy } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_social_democracy = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = conservatism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_conservatism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = liberalism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_liberalism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = authoritarianism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_authoritarianism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = dictatorship } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_dictatorship = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = fascism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fascism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
if = { limit = { has_government = fundamentalism } add_to_variable = { APDC_cabinet_available_fundamentalism = APDC_cabinet_remaining_seats } }
APDC_cabinet_refresh_selected_candidate = yes
APDC_rebuild_cabinet_pie = yes
}
